THIRD-PARTY LICENCES
====================

Tessera is licensed under the Apache License 2.0 (see LICENSE). It is built
from the third-party software below, each under its own licence. This file
holds their licence texts and copyright notices, as those licences ask. Where
a package offers a choice of licences, Tessera uses it under the one named
after "used under". THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md explains what is where, and lists
software that Tessera downloads from its makers instead of bundling it.

Generated by scripts/licenses.mjs from Cargo.lock,
apps/tessera/ui/package-lock.json and the bundled files it names. Don't edit
it by hand: run `node scripts/licenses.mjs`.

Packages: 619 Rust, 1 Rust (vendored), 41 JavaScript, 6 Bundled.
Part 1: 299 licence texts and notices, with the packages that ship each one.
Part 2: 40 packages that ship no licence file of their own.
Part 3: the standard texts of BSD-3-Clause, MIT, MPL-2.0, for part 2.


PART 1. LICENCE TEXTS AND NOTICES

  on a case-by-case basis by examining the author of each
  portion of a file in the revision-control commit records
  of the project, or by consulting representative comments
  claiming copyright ownership for a file.

  For example, the text:

      "Copyright (c) 2011 Google Inc."

  appears in some files, and these files thereby denote
  that their author and copyright-holder is Google Inc.

  In all such cases, the absence of explicit licensing text
  indicates that the contributor chose to license their work
  for distribution under identical terms to those Mozilla
  has chosen for the collective work, enumerated at the top
  of this file. The only difference is the retention of
  copyright itself, held by the contributor.

Contributor are reinstated on an ongoing basis if such Contributor
notifies You of the non-compliance by some reasonable means, this is the

from such Contributor, and You become compliant prior to 30 days after
